# Conformance re-derivation register

## C2 selected amendment and applied migration

The owner selected the ordinary interfaces in `DESIGN.md` and `DECISIONS.md`;
C2 deletes PAR-3 outright. The current sources and manifest implement the
following derivation from that decision. The original C1 inventory is retained
below with its immutable baseline links; its verdict column describes that
baseline, not the amended language.

All 779 original cases remain. Program-kind markers are removed; pure drivers
are ordinary parameterless functions, store drivers name an ordinary Heap
region, and drivers that use invocation values destructure ordinary Inputs.
The native launcher supports these selected signatures as build adapters;
source-only acceptance of another main signature makes no launcher claim.
The false-requires and recursive-main cases are deliberately not executed.
Malformed grammar negatives retain their offending construct. Sole-region
nominal fields retain PROV-1 elision; function/provider positions now write
store brands explicitly. No implicit entry region supplies missing arguments.

The 59 cases using host operations retain their real fixtures, byte and exit
observations. They call ordinary prelude signatures, borrow the factory for
acquisition and explicit consuming close, and match ordinary Result endpoints.
An ordinary borrowed exercise helper may retain its early returns while its
caller closes the original cwd after it returns. This adds no impossible-case
branch. ReadFile, DirectoryRead, DirectorySource and the TCP owners must be
consumed under PROV-6. Permit-reservation branches disappear because that API
was removed, while actual acquisition refusal remains an observed outcome.
The native engine gives no permission to overlap source calls; common factory
state on read_at/read_next/write_once accounts for stream and file aliasing.

The pre-merge C2 structure gate reported 25 runner tests and 130/130 active
rules covered. The combined v0.56 publication also retires META-6 under main's
approved deletion of the derivation ledger; that coverage record is removed
with its rule. These older counts do not report the merged revision's gate.
The canonical adapter and native host CI report execution against their exact
revision. This register records rule grounds, not a substitute gate result.

The main integration at `d17e7e0d` retains all eleven range-loan cases and
their manifest rows unchanged: VIEW-2 endpoint bounds, OWN-5 parent exclusion
and range separation, OWN-6 strength, OWN-10 result lifetime, and PAR-2 runtime
stride permission. Their only source migration is `command fn main` to
ordinary `fn main`, under C2's removal of FN-7's program-kind distinction.
No range assertion, source verdict or expected execution is changed.

The native adapter reuses identical compiled library object bytes within its
test process; every case still compiles its own module and links every native
body with the same options. New object files are removed before execution,
preserving fixture-visible directory contents. This changes build cost only:
case selection, source verdicts, arrangements and exit expectations are
unchanged. The measured ground is in `BACKEND-TESTS.md`.

The grammar binary's census is 86 productions, 136 decisions and 111 terminal
predicates. C2 removes `program_kind`, `input_label`, their two optional use
sites, and the `command`/`as` terminals. Its
`changed_input_label_spelling_fails_closed` assertion is retired with those
grammar productions; mutating an absent rule would not test the active grammar.
The ordinary grammar-mutation checks and parser's
`retired_entry_kind_and_input_labels_are_not_grammar` rejection remain.

### Changed source verdicts

The acquisition-result refinement of PRE-1 replaces the six binary outcome
enums with ordinary Result and adds the ordinary AcceptedConnection success
struct. All affected case sources match Ok/Err; accept cases destructure that
success struct before using its connection and peer. The original successful
owners, refusal handling, linear closes, fixtures and expected verdicts are
preserved. No verdict is changed by this refinement. The runnable program
and embedded-test migrations use the same transformation; the former
`wfgrep` and `dir_walk` Result-conversion helpers now forward the Result.
The selection ground is [Acquisition results](DECISIONS.md#acquisition-results).

The maintained `wfgrep-double-walk` replay sources use ordinary `MutSlice`
parameters for element-only append, copy and diagnostic assembly, and `Slice`
for publication. EFF-2 therefore does not claim whole-buffer descriptor writes
at those calls. This avoids relying on an affine length theorem after ENT-6's
conservative join; it introduces no runtime proof guard or stronger inference.
The inherited algorithms and manifest are unchanged. The original performance
cohort remains frozen; compiling these sources does not renew its measurements.

Each change below is selected by the amended rule, before compiler execution.
All other original expectations, including the exact native exit codes, remain.

The original false requirement is preserved and gives ordinary FN-8 uninhabited acceptance. The case is not executed and the launcher must never fabricate a proof of its requirement. |

### Retired coverage-only assertions

`GATE-1`, `LEDGER-1`, `PATH-1`, `PATH-2`, `HOST-1`, `HOST-2`, `HOST-3`, `SYS-7`, `SYS-8`, `EFF-5`, `QUAL-1`, `QUAL-2`, `QUAL-3`, `GATE-2`, `PAR-3` are removed from the
coverage annotations because their owning rules are deleted. No still-active
rule loses its coverage. SYS/QUAL/HOST/PATH declaration, representation and
native behavior cases now cite PRE-1 and the ordinary ownership, effects,
contracts or enum rule they exercise. Their library fixtures remain useful
implementation evidence; they do not reinstall a language-specific host rule.

expectation and explanation are authoritative. This avoids losing its history
by renaming it into an unrelated passing case.

## Compiler test re-derivation

`compiler/src/semantic/tests/system_effects.rs` (3,407 outgoing lines) is
superseded by `ordinary_effects.rs`. Assertions about `CheckedResultStateOrigin`,
`borrowed_state_origins`, state-history effect unions and release-table rows are
retired under FN-1/EFF-2/STOR-3. They cannot be retained as assertions on a
replacement summary because C2 deletes the mechanism itself. The ordinary
source tests retained below continue to check exact rows, ownership, nested
reborrows, generic replacement, counted run proofs, immutable capacity and the
owning sparse rehash loop. Missing explicit close now tests PROV-6; explicit
close effects test the ordinary EFF-2 row. No source rejection is replaced with
an unsupported or backend outcome.

`entry_form.rs` (426 outgoing lines) is rewritten: FN-7 no longer restricts a
name, root, result, generic header, contract, caller or labelled-input order.
The replacements positively check ordinary main signatures, no main, generic
main, a branded Heap argument, source calls and main requirements; EFF-2 and
GRAM-11 negatives remain ordinary checks. The former program-kind/input-label
diagnostic location assertions are retired with the grammar productions.

The dedicated files `target_action.rs` (201 lines), `staged_permission.rs`
(2,241 lines), and `staged_permission_corpus.rs` (1,731 lines) are deleted.
Their assertions selected or inspected target classes, completion milestones,
staged grants/denials or replicated-window layouts. EFF-3/PAR-1/PAR-2 no longer
carry those premises and PAR-3 is deleted outright. The eight P1 corpus
programs remain ordinary acceptance/execution witnesses in the table above;
ordinary sibling and loop permission tests remain in their own modules.
Deleting these assertions does not certify native overlap behavior or replace
lost PAR-3 performance with a new permission path.

At the initial migration checkpoint, the temporary ordinary `compile` probe
verified the three added conformance witnesses (two accepted, missing-brand
FORM-8). Backend and permission test integration still prevented a full Rust
test run then; that failed interim build is not successful validation.

tests. The following assertions are retired under the named amended rule;
ordinary source outcomes, ownership, bounds, allocator cleanup and native
library behavior remain tested by the retained suites.

The driver's six entry-kind/input-label rejection assertions are retired under
FN-7/PRE-1. Their conformance cases remain in the ordinary acceptance suite.
The semantic delayed-endpoint assertion no longer receives ENT-3.S10's special
I/O fact: it is re-derived as a negative under CALL-4's ordinary deferred
postcondition transport, while the direct-match endpoint positive remains.
The native program suite's reverse-four-peer and staged-lane shape assertions
are retired with PAR-3. Ordinary TCP behavior still executes through both
native library routes; ordinary function declarations replace assertions that
the compiler emits individual TCP operation bodies.

`deterministic_target.rs` retains sixteen executing or layout assertions.
Explicit closes still attempt once, provisional cleanup preserves the selected
error, short transfers preserve absolute endpoints, zero progress returns
WriteZero, and retryable interruptions remain internal to the linked body.
Opaque affine output drop is empty under PRE-1; cwd now closes explicitly, so
the argument-access fixture observes one close rather than an implicit-release
side effect. `parallel.rs` retains the heap-box loop and its exact release
ledger, ordinary multiworker folds, aggregate results and forced lane refusal.
The new worker-helper test calls write_once through an ordinary source body;
no suspension classification authorizes it.

Initial migration evidence: ordinary_effects 23/23, entry_form 8/8,
scripted linked library 16/16, ordinary parallel 29/29, private completion
engine 8/8, and all 80 pinned diagnostic probes passed. The later 98-test
array, buffer, owner-place, reborrow, slice, arena-boundary and source-cost
subset also passed. That snapshot precedes the final directory three-result
API update. These historical subsets do not establish the final revision's
canonical make check or native CI result; the PR reports those separately.


`compiler/src/backend/tests/cost_shape.rs` retains its two source-program tests:
buffer initialization reuse and observed output batching. The following
QUAL-1..3/SYS-5 wrapper and descriptor-publication inspection assertions retire
because the compiler no longer emits per-operation native bodies. Their
solely supporting IR inspection functions are deleted with them. Native
operation behavior remains in the scripted linked-library tests.

### Retained backend source migrations

The retained aggregate/array regressions keep their values, refusal outcomes
and exact allocation ledgers. Their source changes follow the ordinary rules:

| Test file / test | Rule and source migration |
|---|---|
| arrays / recursive_full_arrays_merge_without_a_postcondition_summary | EFF-2 removes writes(out) after out moves into a local destination. Input element reads remain. |
| arrays / full_arrays_preserve_heap_and_arena_element_ownership_through_generic_helpers | EFF-2 removes reads(owner) for ownership passed into a new local record and reads(values) after relay moves its argument to a local conversion; ownership and cleanup observations remain. |
| arrays / heap_full_arrays_preserve_elements_across_calls_replacement_and_refusal | FORM-8 writes the Box store brand in the read helper. |
| owned_places / consumed_result_fields_preserve_padding_siblings_and_smaller_returns | EFF-2 includes whole-parameter reads/writes when the counted loop moves and reinitializes the complete Row, beside actual field accesses. |
| owned_places / ordinary_box_owner_transfer_keeps_values_and_release_across_two_helpers | EFF-2 removes reads(incoming) after its transfer into local replacement state. The current owner and store effects remain. |
| owned_places / partial_construction_refusal_preserves_effects_values_and_release_order | EFF-2 removes former value.first/value.second effects after destructuring the owner into local cells; explicit store cleanup still writes(store). |
| owned_places / value_if_cleans_unchosen_owners_before_reusing_delivery_storage | EFF-2 removes original-parameter field reads after moving the selected owner to a local binding. Store cleanup remains. |
| owned_places / addressed_owner_cleanup_releases_descriptor_fields_in_checked_order | EFF-2 whole-value dispose writes the complete owner; the direct stamp access remains field-specific. |
| owned_places / box_field_borrows_keep_the_owner_slot_across_reborrow_and_return | FORM-8 spells the shared/exclusive result loan region independently of the Box store region. |
| buffers / generic_allocation_fit_uses_each_concrete_element_ceiling | FORM-8 names the Vector store region. The nonallocating main is pure and takes an ordinary Heap solely to name that brand. |
| buffers / run_cleanup_is_explicit_on_return_and_break_edges | FORM-8 writes the helper Heap store brand. |
| reborrows / opaque_resource_borrows_write_back_through_calls_fields_and_reborrows | EFF-2 removes the original file parameter's effects after constructing a local Holder. The C observer uses the ordinary 32-byte, align-16 opaque destination ABI and checks every transferred word plus both adjacent scalar fields. |
| reborrows / opaque_aggregate_borrows_preserve_replacement_and_linked_observations | FORM-8 explicitly names the borrowed HostString result's parameter region. The original byte-content observations remain. |
| slices / a_view_of_a_frame_resident_run_reaches_its_own_slots_until_call_return | OWN-6/VIEW-2 preserve the complete inline-storage observation through an ordinary linked call; the obsolete suspension-class name and description retire with PAR-3. |
| ordinary retained-call helpers and symbol assertions | PRE-1 one callable ABI emits public WF definitions and ordinary ExitStatus result destinations; no internal/system symbol spelling remains required. Monomorphized function assertions select two exact signatures independently of PRE declaration ordinals. |

Canonical blank lines between declarations are restored in embedded source
fixtures. Bounds-negative values, return values and execution observations are
unchanged; formatting no longer masks the operation the regression tests.

The allocation-shape test distinguishes a retained public helper definition
from an uninlined call to it: PRE-1's ordinary public ABI can preserve the
former after every call is inlined. Each definition still contains exactly
one allocation, every retained call resolves to such a definition, and the
eleven source allocations retain the same exact byte-extent ledger.

### Pinned diagnostic sentences

`compiler/src/driver/pinned_sentences.rs` keeps 80 source probes and their
exact ordinary diagnostic fragments. FN-7 removes each `command` scaffold.
The `DirectoryRead` collision is re-derived as the same TYPE-6/PRE-1
collision as `Option`; the range witness now passes an ordinary view and
checks FN-8/CALL-1's instantiated requirement. The excessive region argument
to `args_count` now checks the ordinary FORM-8 sentence. The shared-borrow
mismatch uses an ordinary function with the same exclusive parameter type.
Only `system-argument-does-not-name-a-region.wf` and its SYS-2-specific
sentence retire: that separate argument class no longer exists, while the
ordinary FN-2 arity and FORM-8 region diagnostics remain pinned. The three
PAR-3 notice sentences mentioned by the old module introduction retire with
the staging report, as recorded above.

## C1 baseline inventory (historical evidence)

Baseline `20043721`. Every case in `tests/conformance/manifest.jsonl` appears
once below. Links identify source and manifest lines at that revision.
This table records the pre-amendment sources, verdicts and proposed migration categories. The applied C2 derivation above supersedes pending choices; these baseline links remain unchanged evidence.

The base cases deliberately exercise many unrelated language rules. E1 is
their common scaffold migration, not a claim that those rules are external.
All ordinary negatives must still reach the stated rule after their scaffold
is migrated; an earlier linearity error is not a substitute for a range or
borrow failure the case is intended to test.

## Migration key

| Code | Rule change and concrete migration |
| --- | --- |
| E1 | GRAM-2/FN-7/PROG-3: use an ordinary function entry and ordinary launcher arguments/results. Replace labelled inputs by selected ordinary fields/parameters. Retain the case's independent semantic assertion and expected output; do not blindly replace tokens inside a deliberately malformed construct. |
| E2 | FN-7 entry-only rule removed: re-derive missing-marker, input-label, main-call and main-contract cases. Missing marker and source call are ordinary positives; a well-formed contradictory main contract is ordinary FN-8 uninhabited code, not a callable program with a fabricated proof. Label syntax no longer exists; retire the old ordinal/type-label rejection and test ordinary fields/signatures instead. |
| A1 | SYS/HOST/PATH semantics move to ordinary API: exact ordinary parameter types, explicit view construction, selected outcomes, complete linear close/return on all paths, and direct host-state effects. Keep native fixture and output checks as library integration evidence; ordinary nominal/ownership assertions remain compiler conformance. |
| N1 | SYS-1/SYS-3/TYPE-6: re-home collisions and near-lookalikes in the ordinary Prelude domain, changing the conflicting origin/rank to PRE-1 while preserving ordinary name resolution. Kernel-row collision remains BLK-0/TYPE-6. |
| R1 | SYS-8/ENT-3.S10 removed: put the two range requirements and available Result.Ok bounds in fn_sig contracts. Retain the rejected start/end values; the negative now proves an ordinary FN-8 call requirement failure. Runtime outputs test ordinary linked body contracts. |
| L1 | SYS-5/STOR-3/EFF-2 implicit native release removed: retire the automatic-close/release-effect assertion. Add explicit consuming close with its declared host-state parameter, plus a PROV-6 negative for leaving a selected linear owner unconsumed. An affine opaque empty drop carries no finalizer or writes(owner). |
| Q1 | QUAL-1..3 removed: preserve the native behavior/ABI fixture as an ordinary build/library test; remove semantic-ID/qualification acceptance authority. An unavailable linked definition is not a source-language rejection. |
| P1 | PAR-3 removed, PAR-1/PAR-2 target summaries removed: preserve source legality/output after API migration. Retire staged-grant/numbered-denial assertions with the rule change; keep ordinary interference/loan checks and record lost overlap separately. |
| T1 | SYS-2/SYS-18 special struct construction removed: use ordinary public fields and a total explicit half-close API (recommended), or the owner's chosen ordinary opaque representation. With public fields, construction is admitted; test cross-paired halves. Preserve genuine move/borrow/field-effect negatives. |
| O1 | FN-1/EFF-2 owned-routing removed: keep the ordinary Box replacement/result values and provider reclamation checks; rewrite exact rows against storage places, without historical input/result aliases. No ABI result change is implied. |
| O2 | FN-1/EFF-2 installed-owner input-read requirement removed: `eff2-neg-installed-owner-read-omitted` becomes a storage-place positive after entry migration; `reads(owner), writes(owner, store)` suffices. Retire the historical missing-`reads(incoming)` rejection explicitly. |
| T2 | SYS-2 constructor exception removed, but this exact case supplies `unit` for both direction fields. It must still reject ordinary field type mismatch (TYPE-5); add a separate well-typed construction/cross-pair positive, do not flip this verdict to accept. |
| F1 | FN-4 fresh-result routing restriction removed: retire that binding rejection and add an ordinary positive returning an input owner. Keep mismatched signatures/rows/contracts as negatives; ordinary borrow provenance is unchanged. |
| B1 | BLK-4/FN-7 entry-heap selector removed: use an ordinary supplied store brand/region and re-derive availability/confinement under PROV-1/PROV-6, rather than checking a command label. |
| K1 | No external-only premise detected beyond the scaffold: keep the stated ordinary rule; re-run the exact source after E1. This includes source borrow suspension and proof entry-image tracking, neither of which is FN-1 owned-state routing. |

## Non-case coverage records

Coverage exemptions are part of the evidence and must migrate too. A deleted
language rule loses its active coverage row; its useful native fixture remains
under ordinary library tests. Ordinary proof/layout/diagnostic policies keep
coverage without the special domain references.
